## `AFG-0146-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Egg Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Egg Candling & Day-Old Chick Sexing Quality
- **Intro:** Egg Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Egg Candling & Day-Old Chick Sexing Quality — Pre-Shift Calibration Verification: Ambient temperature 20-25°C, relative humidity 40-60%, power supply 220V AC ±10%, lux meter calibration certificate valid within 12 months.
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature 20-25°C, relative humidity 40-60%, power supply 220V AC ±10%, lux meter calibration certificate valid within 12 months.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO 22000:2018`, `Afghanistan National Standards Authority (ANSA) - Poultry Produc`, `Chapter 7.5`
